# 🎯 Topical & Conceptual Index — Torah Book Typesetting System

> This index organizes all documentation **by subject and concepts** (not by file). The structure is top-down: from system purpose → architecture → core engines → features → issues → comparison of approaches between different versions. Each item links directly to the exact location in the source file.

## 🗺 How to use this index

- Each topic is presented with **nested sub-topics** at different levels.
- Each item has **2 links**: a heading anchor (opens the file at that point) and a line number (`L42`).
- Where different versions (Claude/ChatGPT/z.ai) take different approaches, this is explicitly noted in the **"Comparison of Approaches"** section.

> **Note:** Source files are in Hebrew. Link text shows the original Hebrew heading (as it appears in the source). English descriptions are provided for each section.

---

## 1. Top Level — What the System Is and Why

### 1.1 System Purpose and the Problem It Solves

A web-based typesetting system for Torah (religious scholarly) books — two-column body text, spanning headings, footnotes and side notes, while adhering to traditional Torah printing rules.

- [`ייעוד המערכת`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/comparisons/Analysis_Comparison_Versions_System_Typesetting.md#%D7%99%D7%99%D7%A2%D7%95%D7%93-%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%A2%D7%A8%D7%9B%D7%AA) ([line](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/comparisons/Analysis_Comparison_Versions_System_Typesetting.md#L1157)) — `shared/comparisons/Analysis_Comparison_Versions_System_Typesetting.md`

### 1.2 Typographic Background and Complexity of the Torah Book

The Torah book presents challenges not found in regular literature: multi-text, Berech (L-shape) layout, spanning headings, absolute vertical balancing, widow/orphan prevention, and multi-column footnotes.

- [`הקדמה ורקע טיפוגרפי`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/research/Torah_typesetting_overview.md#%D7%94%D7%A7%D7%93%D7%9E%D7%94-%D7%95%D7%A8%D7%A7%D7%A2-%D7%98%D7%99%D7%A4%D7%95%D7%92%D7%A8%D7%A4%D7%99) ([line](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/research/Torah_typesetting_overview.md#L11)) — `shared/research/Torah_typesetting_overview.md`

### 2.2 Architectural Pattern: Immediate-Mode Rebuild

The system is not MVC/MVVM — the source of truth is the input text. Every change triggers a full rebuild of pages.

- [`1.4 תבנית ארכיטקטונית: בנייה מחדש מלאה (Immediate-Mode Rebuild)`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/claude_v20/technical/Technical_specification.md#14-%D7%AA%D7%91%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%AA-%D7%90%D7%A8%D7%9B%D7%99%D7%98%D7%A7%D7%98%D7%95%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%AA-%D7%91%D7%A0%D7%99%D7%99%D7%94-%D7%9E%D7%97%D7%93%D7%A9-%D7%9E%D7%9C%D7%90%D7%94-immediate-mode-rebuild) ([line](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/claude_v20/technical/Technical_specification.md#L56)) — `claude_v20/technical/Technical_specification.md`

### 2.3 Zero-Backend Pure Web (Security)

The system runs entirely in the browser — no server, no file uploads, all processing is local.

The system consists of 5 core files: `index.html`, `style.css`, `app.js`, `decorations.js`, `typesetter.js`.

### 2.6 Information Flow: Tag (Batch) vs. V2 (Reactive In-Memory)

⚠️ **Comparison of approaches**: The 'Tag' software works in a Batch Pipeline (Word → codes → engine → printed page), while the V2 engine works in Reactive In-Memory mode (live editor → layers 1-2-3 → A4 DOM).

- [`השוואת זרימת המידע (Pipeline Comparison): תג מול מנוע Master V2`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/comparisons/Tag_comparison.md#%D7%94%D7%A9%D7%95%D7%95%D7%90%D7%AA-%D7%96%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%9E%D7%AA-%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%99%D7%93%D7%A2-pipeline-comparison-%D7%AA%D7%92-%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%9C-%D7%9E%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%A2-master-v2) ([line](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/comparisons/Tag_comparison.md#L60)) — `shared/comparisons/Tag_comparison.md`

## 3. Core Engines — Three Layers

⚠️ **Comparison of approaches**: All three versions (Claude/ChatGPT/z.ai) describe a similar three-layer architecture, but emphasize different parts. The sections below link to all descriptions.

### 3.1 Layer 1 — DFA AST Parser (Parsing & Syntax)

Deterministic O(N) scan of the input text. Isolates metadata to the header only, and decomposes nested footnotes `[...]` with algorithmic precision.

- **Claude — AST parsing**: [`3. מנגנון State Machine ו-AST לזיהוי קידומות ללא נגיעה ב-DOM`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/archive/reports/Audit_report.md#3-%D7%9E%D7%A0%D7%92%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%9F-state-machine-%D7%95-ast-%D7%9C%D7%96%D7%99%D7%94%D7%95%D7%99-%D7%A7%D7%99%D7%93%D7%95%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%AA-%D7%9C%D7%9C%D7%90-%D7%A0%D7%92%D7%99%D7%A2%D7%94-%D7%91-dom) ([L](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/archive/reports/Audit_report.md#L380)) — `archive/reports/Audit_report.md` · [`עדכנתי באותו קומיט גם את `worklog.md` ואת `V23/MASTER_WORK_REGISTER.md`, ל…`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/chatgpt_v23/transcripts/Chat_08.md#%D7%A2%D7%93%D7%9B%D7%A0%D7%AA%D7%99-%D7%91%D7%90%D7%95%D7%AA%D7%95-%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%9E%D7%99%D7%98-%D7%92%D7%9D-%D7%90%D7%AA-worklogmd-%D7%95%D7%90%D7%AA-v23masterworkregistermd-%D7%9C%D7%A8%D7%91%D7%95%D7%AA-%D7%AA%D7%95%D7%A6%D7%90%D7%95%D7%AA-%D7%94%D7%91%D7%93%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%94-%D7%94%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%93%D7%9E%D7%AA-%D7%94%D7%9E%D7%92%D7%91%D7%9C%D7%94-%D7%A9%D7%A0%D7%9E%D7%A6%D7%90%D7%94-%D7%94%D7%A9%D7%99%D7%A0%D7%95%D7%99-%D7%A9%D7%91%D7%95%D7%A6%D7%A2-%D7%95%D7%94%D7%A1%D7%98%D7%98%D7%95%D7%A1-%D7%A9%D7%98%D7%A8%D7%9D-%D7%90%D7%95%D7%9E%D7%AA) ([L](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/chatgpt_v23/transcripts/Chat_08.md#L1855)) — `chatgpt_v23/transcripts/Chat_08.md`

### 3.2 Layer 2 — Knuth-Plass (Micro-typography & Line Breaking)

Total-Fit algorithm for optimal line breaking per paragraph. In-memory Canvas-based measurement, penalties for breaking after acronyms/gereshayim, first-word window (drop cap) locked to baseline grid.

### 3.3 Layer 3 — Multi-Column DP (Macro-typography)

Dynamic programming for page balancing and chapter-end balancing. Prevents orphan lines, balances column heights by whole lines, reserves multi-page footnote budget without overflow.

### 3.4 Integration Bridge — performTypeset_V2

The main function that unifies the three layers into a single rendering pipeline. Replaces the old `performTypeset` function.

## 4. System Features and Functionality

### 4.1 Page Geometry and Column Grid

A4 page (210×297mm), 15mm padding, 180×267mm content area. 7-column grid: margins-gap-column-gutter-column-gap-margins.

### 4.2 Heading Modes (H0–H7)

Each heading level (0 to 7) has a mode selector: `sub_shaar`, `new_page_1col`, `span_1col`, etc. The mode determines which block the heading creates and how it affects page division.

### 4.6 Running Headers and Page Numbering

Support for Recto/Verso (right/left page separation), dynamic text variables, gematria (Hebrew numeral) page numbering, catchword (last-word-of-page repeated).

### 4.7 Fonts

Support for OpenType, TrueType, WOFF2, Google Fonts. Font list loaded from `fonts/fonts.json`. HarfBuzz for niqqud (vowel points) and ta'amei hamikra (cantillation marks) stabilization.

### 5.2 Widows, Orphans, and Orphaned Headings

In typography: "widow" = last line of a paragraph at the top of the next column/page; "orphan" = first line of a paragraph at the bottom of the previous column/page.

### 5.3 Chapter-End Column Balancing

Classic problem: chapter end leaves the right column full and the left column empty. The algorithm must balance both columns to the same horizontal line.

### 5.4 Font Issues — Tofu, Fallback, U+E000

Missing characters display as empty boxes (Tofu) or squares (U+E000). Requires `detectTofu` algorithm and proper fallback.

- **Tofu (□ missing glyph boxes)**: [`10. **כל התווים מוצגים** — אין "תיבות ריקות" (Tofu □) או ריבועים במקום תוו…`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/specs/Software_specification.md#10-%D7%9B%D7%9C-%D7%94%D7%AA%D7%95%D7%95%D7%99%D7%9D-%D7%9E%D7%95%D7%A6%D7%92%D7%99%D7%9D--%D7%90%D7%99%D7%9F-%D7%AA%D7%99%D7%91%D7%95%D7%AA-%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%AA-tofu--%D7%90%D7%95-%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%91%D7%95%D7%A2%D7%99%D7%9D-%D7%91%D7%9E%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%9D-%D7%AA%D7%95%D7%95%D7%99%D7%9D-%D7%94%D7%92%D7%95%D7%A4%D7%9F-%D7%94%D7%A0%D7%91%D7%97%D7%A8-%D7%9E%D7%9B%D7%99%D7%9C-%D7%90%D7%AA-%D7%9B%D7%9C-%D7%94%D7%AA%D7%95%D7%95%D7%99%D7%9D-%D7%91%D7%98%D7%A7%D7%A1%D7%98) ([L](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/specs/Software_specification.md#L119)) — `shared/specs/Software_specification.md` · [`3.7 ריבועים במקום תווים (Tofu ו-U+E000)`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/specs/Software_specification.md#37-%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%91%D7%95%D7%A2%D7%99%D7%9D-%D7%91%D7%9E%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%9D-%D7%AA%D7%95%D7%95%D7%99%D7%9D-tofu-%D7%95-ue000) ([L](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/specs/Software_specification.md#L170)) — `shared/specs/Software_specification.md` · [`8.12 בעיה 12: Tofu (תיבות ריקות)`](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/specs/Software_specification.md#812-%D7%91%D7%A2%D7%99%D7%94-12-tofu-%D7%AA%D7%99%D7%91%D7%95%D7%AA-%D7%A8%D7%99%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%AA) ([L](https://github.com/SUGINACH/Typesetter_doc/blob/main/shared/specs/Software_specification.md#L700)) — `shared/specs/Software_specification.md`

### 5.5 Failure Log, Mistakes, and Rejected Attempts

⚠️ **Especially important material**: Detailed list of approaches that were tried and failed, including reasons for failure. Essential for avoiding repetition of mistakes.

### 7.3 RTL Justification in Hebrew

In traditional Torah books, end-of-line hyphenation is rare — the engine must achieve full justification almost exclusively by stretching/compressing spaces.

## 8. Version and Tool Comparisons

### 8.1 The Three Versions — Claude / ChatGPT / z.ai

⚠️ **Key approach comparison**: The project was developed in parallel by three AI tools. Each used a different approach:

- 📘 **Claude (Yellow/IMUD_TORANI)**: Conservative and methodical approach, emphasis on precise specifications and one massive review file (V20.4 with 11,713 lines). Focuses on V20.1–V20.5 version specifications.
- 💚 **ChatGPT (Green V23)**: Distributed approach with clear separation between management (MASTER_WORK_REGISTER), research (RESEARCH_DETERMINISTIC_TYPESSETTING_ENGINE), and technical documentation (TECHNICAL_HISTORY_AND_ARCHITECTURE). 10 full chat transcripts.
- 🥇 **z.ai (Gold V21)**: Combination of academic research (Knuth-Plass research reports) with detailed English technical documentation. Strong emphasis on CI/CD and GitHub Actions.

### 8.2 Comparison with 'Tag' Software

'Tag' software (developed by Daniel Weissman) is the industry standard for Torah typesetting. Comparison covers architecture, capabilities, cost (4,920 NIS + add-ons), and gaps (Talmud page layout / multi-text).
